# Deleted Checkpoints
All PyTorch/model checkpoint files under `research/` were inventoried with SHA256 before deletion. They were removed because the experiments are reproducible from retained runners, configuration, seeds, git SHA, and canonical numerical results.
Checkpoint count: **670**; total bytes: **333,920,370**.
